買進 
Price(09/14)：323.5 
Target Price：420

上銀 26Q2 受惠於產品漲價效益顯現及 UTR 提升，單季毛利率攀
升至 34.17%。單季合併營收達 75.08 億元，QoQ 17.70% 、YoY 
26.71%。營業利益與稅後淨利分別大幅成長至 10.34 億元與 8.48
億元，帶動單季 EPS 攀升至 2.40 元。 
展望後市， 研究部對上銀營收表現維持正向看待 。 目前終端需求持
續暢旺，產品交期維持高位，線性滑軌能見度約 4 至 5 個月，滾
珠螺桿最長可達 6 個月 。 儘管地緣政治因素推升鋼材 、 包材與潤滑
油等原物料價格 ， 但公司具備彈性定價能力且維持多元採購策略，
漲價效益足以抵銷成本上揚。伴隨26Q3 完整反映漲價產品銷售，
預估單季營收達 80.92 億元，QoQ 7.77%，YoY 34.97%，EPS 上
看 2.81 元。放眼 2026 至 2027 年，亞洲半導體需求穩固、歐洲軍
工與航太產業接棒成為新動能 ， 整體訂單需求無虞 。 預估上銀2026
年與 2027 年營收將分別達 296.90 億元與 329.36 億元 ， 維持雙位
數年增幅，YoY 分別為 22.37%與 10.93%。在營運規模提升帶動
下，預估 2026 年、2027 年 EPS 將分別達 9.28 元與 11.87 元。 
評價方面，上銀過去五年 PER 主要集中於 20 倍至 40 倍區間。考
量公司營收與獲利將呈現雙位數成長上升趨勢，產業結構持續上
行，研究部以2027 年預估 EPS 11.87 元作為評價基礎，並給予歷
史區間中緣偏上之 35x 本益比，目標價為 420 元，給予「買進」
之投資建議。  
⚫   ⚫ 公司基本資料 
上銀科技(2049)成立於 1989 年，主要從事線性滑軌(GW)、滾
螺桿(BS)、工業機器人、機器手臂、迴轉工作台、減速機、精
密軸承及醫療設備之研究開發、設計、製造、銷售、維修及售
前售後服務，同時為全球第三大滾珠螺桿廠和線性滑軌廠，僅
次於日本 THK、NSK。公司以自有品牌 「HIWIN」 行銷全球，
海外子公司橫跨德國、美國、日本、瑞士、捷克、法國，

⚫ 漲價效益浮現+UTR 提升，26Q2 毛利率攀升至 34.17% 
上銀 26Q2 單季 EPS 攀升至 2.40 元。自 5、6 月起，產品
漲價效益逐漸顯現，加上亞洲市場需求延續帶動交期延長；其
中 ， 機器人產品受惠於半導體需求強勁 ， 出貨量年增達 7 成。
第二季合併營收達 75.08 億元 ，QoQ 17.70%，YoY 26.71%。 
獲利表現方面，受惠於漲價效益與 UTR 提升，單季毛利率達

券餘(張) 156 57 34.17%，季增 2.22 個百分點，年增 4.43 個百分點。在營
收與毛利率雙成長帶動下，營業利益大幅攀升至  10.34 億

⚫ 預估營收獲利季增，帶動 26Q3 單季 EPS 達 2.81 元 
上銀 8 月營收 27.08 億元 ，MoM 0.98%，YoY 42.30%；1-8
月累計營收 192.77 億元，YoY 23.03% 。研究部預期，9 月
營收有望延續雙位數年增態勢，預估  26Q3 營收將達 80.92 
億元，QoQ 7.77%，YoY 34.97%。 
獲利表現方面，考量第三季將完整反映產品漲價效益，且營收
規 模 擴 張 帶 動  UTR 提 升 ， 預 估  26Q3 毛 利 率 將 達 
35.43%，季增 1.27 個百分點，年增 6.83 個百分點。預估
單季營業利益達 12.00 億元 ，QoQ 16.11%，YoY 192.40%；
稅後淨利 9.94 億元，QoQ 17.25% ，YoY 122.90% ，單季 
EPS 預估為 2.81 元。

⚫ 全球需求延續，獲利有望持續成長，估 2026 年 EPS 9.28 元 
研究部對上銀後市營收維持正向看待，係因以下：(1) 終端需
求持續暢旺，產品交期維持高位； (2) 公司具備彈性定價能
力，漲價效益足以覆蓋成本上揚；(3) 半導體產業需求穩固，
持續帶動滾珠螺桿與機器人等相關產品出貨。 
訂單能見度方面，目前整體產品交期維持高檔，線性滑軌約  
4-5 個月 ， 滾珠螺桿最長可達 6 個月 。 觀察各區域下游需求：
亞洲市場維持暢旺，其中中國受惠於電動車、電子與半導體產
業持續擴張，帶動製造設備與傳動元件需求；東南亞受惠全球
供應鏈重組，除傳統傳動元件外， 亦間接推升機器人產品需
求；台灣則持續受惠半導體與科技業資本支出，帶動螺桿與機
器人穩健成長。歐洲市場方面，儘管德國汽車工業仍顯疲態，
但軍工與航太產業已成為新成長動能，加上捷克、波蘭等中歐
製造核心需求保持穩定，有效支撐歐洲營運。

生產與成本端方面，雖地緣政治因素推升包材、潤滑油、銑刀
及鋼材等原物料價格 ， 但目前產品的漲價幅度已足以抵銷成本
波動，加上公司針對鋼材維持多元採購策略以對衝風險，研究
部預估上銀 2026 至 2027 年毛利率與營業利益將呈穩健成
長態勢。 
綜上所述，隨需求延續，研究部預估上銀 2026/2027 年營收
為 296.90 億 / 329.36 億，YoY 22.37% / 10.93%；伴隨營運
規模提升帶動獲利表現 ， 毛利率預估為 34.08% / 34.39%，年
增 5.32 / 0.31 個百分點；營業利益預估為 38.85 億 / 50.93
億，YoY 136.44% / 31.09%；EPS 預估為 9.28 / 11.87 元。

⚫ 結論與投資建議：
上銀 26Q2 受惠於產品漲價效益顯現及 UTR 提升，單季毛利
率 攀 升 至 34.17% 。 單 季 合 併 營 收 達75.08 億元， QoQ
17.70%、YoY 26.71%。營業利益與稅後淨利分別大幅成長至
10.34 億元與 8.48 億元，帶動單季 EPS 攀升至 2.40 元。
展望後市，研究部對上銀營收表現維持正向看待。目前終端需
求持續暢旺，產品交期維持高位，線性滑軌能見度約 4 至 5
個月，滾珠螺桿最長可達 6 個月。儘管地緣政治因素推升鋼
材、包材與潤滑油等原物料價格，但公司具備彈性定價能力且
維持多元採購策略，漲價效益足以抵銷成本上揚。伴隨 26Q3
完整反映漲價產品銷售，預估單季營收達 80.92 億元，QoQ

7.77%，YoY 34.97%，EPS 上看 2.81 元。

太產業接棒成為新動能，整體訂單需求無虞。預估上銀 2026
年與 2027 年營收將分別達 296.90 億元與 329.36 億元 ， 維持
雙位數年增幅，YoY 分別為 22.37%與 10.93%。在營運規模
提升帶動下，預估 2026 年、2027 年 EPS 將分別達 9.28 元
與 11.87 元。

## Document 045: 2308台達電 兆豐

### Page 1

評等分級：買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操
作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出（預
期未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。 
本刊所刊載之內容僅做為參考，惟已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量
投資之需求與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任。任何轉載或引用本報告內容必須經本公司同意。

- 1 - 
個股報告

台達電(2308) 
缺料與物料問題，創造長線布局機會 
投資評等 目標價 
逢低買進 $2,000 
前次投資建議：2026.07.30 
「買進」，目標價: $2,000

報告日期：2026.09.16 研 究 員：邱岳霖

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 缺料影響營運不如預期，下修 Q3獲利預估，EPS 9元 
➢ HVDC滲透率提升，且持續海內外擴產，2027 年營收挑戰超越 1兆元，EPS 62.3元。考量
電源產業將進入全新格局且台達作為主要先行者將最為受惠，兆豐投顧維持目標價 2000 元
(約 32X 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 19.8%，建議逢低買進。

- 2 - 
個股報告 
資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析

台達電為全球電源管理及散熱解決方案領導廠商 
台達電(2308)成立於 1971 年並於 1988 年掛牌上市，成立初期分別投入生產
交換式電源供應器與無碳刷式直流風扇，目 前已取得全球電源產品龍頭地
位。2017 年公司啟動組織調整，以「電源及零組件」、「自動化」、「基礎
設施」、「交通」四大業務範疇，並從傳統關鍵零組件製造商逐步轉型為節
能解決方案提供者。近年隨AI 資料中心建置需求爆發，公司積極將既有電源
技術延伸至 AI伺服器電源機櫃、液冷 side car、BBU、DC-DC/AC-DC模組
及 CDU 等新產品線，目前終端應用已從傳統 NB/工業電源、資料中心跨足
至高壓供電(HVDC)與液冷散熱系統，客戶涵蓋NVIDIA與北美 CSP及 ASIC
供應鏈。

26Q2 營收比重:(1)電源及零組件 53%,包含電源、被動元件等零組件、風扇
散熱;(2)交通 5%,電動車解決方案;(3)自動化 8%,包含工業自動化,樓宇自動
化;(4)基礎設施 34%,包含資訊通訊技術(通信電源、網通交換器、資料中心
解決方案、液冷side car)、能源基礎設施(電動車充電樁、可再生能源及儲能
設備等)。受惠 AI 資料中心電源需求與規格持續提升，台達持續擴充台灣、
中國大陸、泰國與美國產能，並同步推進 800V HVDC 架構開發，全面迎接
NVIDIA 新世代 GPU 機櫃功耗大幅提升所帶動的電源與散熱升級需求。

- 3 - 
個股報告

缺料影響營運不如預期，下修 Q3獲利預估，EPS 9 元 
台達電(2308)公告 8月營收：營收 645.9 億元，MoM -3.7%/YoY +34.9%，
Q3達成率 62.4%，低於研究部預期與市場共識，係因 MOSFET 全產品線呈
現缺料所致，故研究部下修台達電 26Q3 營收 1960.8 億元(原估 2,107.4 億
元)，QoQ +7.0%/YoY +30.4%，而先前 18.3KW PSU 因電容問題有進行產
品召回，不過預期本月將處理完畢且對財務無任何影響；毛利率方面，受惠
產品組合轉佳與規模經濟抵銷原料/電子元件漲價影響，預估毛利率 35.8%，
仍可維持與上季相當，季增 0.2ppt，反映營收下修，同步下修稅後淨利 232.6
億元，QoQ -7.5%/YoY +25.0%，EPS 9元(原估 10.5 元)。

AI 電源與散熱產品持續放量，輔以新產品推出，2026 EPS 37元 
展望 2026 年，主要成長動能仍來自 AI 資料中心電源、散熱與基礎設施，在
電源方面隨著 GB300 持續放量與 H2 VR200之 18.3KW 產品開始出貨，預
期價量齊揚挹注營運動能，此外 HVDC(±400V/800V)產品於 3Q26 開始量
產，惟在初期階段預期僅小幅貢獻；在散熱產品方面(涵蓋水冷板、Manifold、
CDU及 Sidecar)在 2026 下半年可望延續成長動能，主要受惠液冷滲透率持
續提升與內容價值同步墊高。綜上所述，反映 Q3下修，同步下修台達電 2026
年營運預估：2026 年營收 7,465.4 億元，YoY +34.5%，毛利率 36.2%，年
增 1.9ppt，稅後淨利 960.8 億元，YoY +59.8%，EPS 37.0元（原估 40 元）。

HVDC滲透率提升，且持續海內外擴產，2027年營收挑戰超越 1兆元，EPS 
62.3元 
展望 2027 年，受惠 CSP業者 CAPEX 持續增長，公司為滿足訂單需求，持
續加大投資力道，2026 年資本支出約 700 億元(YoY +50%)，資金來源除自
有資金外，9/15 公告發行泰達電可交換公司債籌措 15 億美元以滿足擴產資
金需求，2027 年也可望維持高檔水準，預計於泰國、中國、台灣與美國同步
擴產，研究部認為 HVDC(±400V/800V)較大規模量產貢獻有望於 2027 年起
顯現，同時液冷、電源機櫃、DC-DC模組等新產品線持續放量，此外公司亦
持續推進 SST 與 SOFC 等能源基礎設施長期布局，電源產業將迎來新格局
與長期成長動能。兆豐投顧預估台達電 2027 年營收 1.1 兆元，YoY +43.0%，
毛利率 37%，年增 0.8ppt，稅後淨利 1617.6 億元，YoY +68.4%，EPS 62.3
元。

## Document 046: 2330台積電 群益

Source PDF: all report pdf/2330台積電 群益.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-22
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

1 群益投顧 
ASML (ASML US) 與台積電 (2330 TT) 共同推動 High NA EUV 將自現行的 6 吋光罩，
轉移至更大的 12 吋 光 罩。 目標於 2031 年之前建立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ， 並 於
2033 年之前實現微影系統全面就緒。台積電並表示自 2030 年 起 ， 將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產
中導入 ASML 的 High NA  EUV 。台積電與 ASML 共同發起 6 吋光罩轉移至 12 吋光
罩 的 規 格 轉 移 ， 其 戰 略 意 義 在 於 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積 電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。 因 此
對 台 積 電 的 投 資 評 等 Buy ，目標價 3,200 元。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

ASML 與 台 積 電 共 同 推 動 High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋 光 罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大
的 12 吋 光 罩 ：  
09/07/2026 ASML 與 台 積 電 於 BACUS 國 際 光 學 工 程 學 會(SPIE) 先 進 微 影 技
術 研 討 會 前 宣 布 發 起 產 業 合 作 ， 共 同 推 動High NA EUV 將 自 現 行 的 6 吋光
罩 ， 轉 移 至 更 大 的 12 吋 光 罩 ， 預 期 將 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 的 生 產 力 、 降 低 晶
片 製 造 成 本 ， 並 消 除 需 要 2 張 6 吋 光 罩 在 High NA EUV 曝 光 時 進 行 拼 接 的
限 制，使 整 個 半 導 體 產 業 受 惠。此 轉 移 能 讓 先 進 晶 片 製 造 服 務 提 供 者 充 分 利
用 High NA EUV 的 優 勢 ， 更 提 升 未 來 世 代 先 進 晶 片 的 成 本 效 益 。 
目標於 2031 年 之 前 建 立 12 吋 光 罩 試 產 線(pilot line) ，並於 2033 年 之 前 實
現 微 影 系 統 全 面 就 緒 ， 以 支 援 先 進 製 程 量 產 。 台 積 電 並 表 示 自2030 年 起 ，
將 在 先 進 製 程 量 產 中 導 入 ASML 的 High NA 技 術 。  
12 吋光罩 使 High NA 效 率 提 升 ：  
現行 EUV 設備的 NA(Numerical Aperture ， NA) 為 0.33 ， High NA 則 提 高 到
0.55 。 NA 數 值 越 高 ， 解 析 度 也 越 高 、 曝 光 時 能 呈 現 的 細 節 愈 多 。ASML 資
料顯示 High NA 可 將 最 小 解 析 尺 寸 從 現 行 EUV 的約 13 奈 米 進 一 步 縮 小 到
8 奈 米 ， 也 就 是 能 在 晶 圓 上 印 出 更 細 微 的 電 路 圖 形 。 部 分 原 本 需 要2 次 甚 至
更多次曝光才能完成的圖形，也有機會改以單次曝光處理，降低製程複雜
度 、 缺 陷 風 險 與 生 產 周 期 。 
舉 例 來 說 ，0.33 NA EUV 機 台 ， 至 少 需 要 4 張 光 罩 、 分 多 次 曝 光 才 能 完 成
A14 、 A10 金 屬 層 圖 形 ；High NA EUV ， 只 要 1 張 光 罩 就 能 完 成 相 同 圖 形 。
但 解 析 度 提 高 ，High NA 的 光 學 設 計 讓 單 次 可 曝 光 範 圍 縮 小 到 原 本EUV 的
50% ， 製 作 大 顆 晶 片 時 可 能 必 須 分 成2 次 曝 光 ， 再 把 圖 形 拼 接 起 來 。 這 也 是
台積電與 ASML 此 次 推 動 12 吋 光 罩 的 原 因 ， 相 較 現 行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 放 大 光 罩
尺 寸 預 期 能 進 一 步 提 升 晶 圓 廠 生 產 力、降 低 晶 片 製 造 成 本，並 消 除 High NA
曝 光 時 的 拼 接 限 制 。 
台 積 電 預 計 2030 年 開 始 使 用 High NA  EUV ， 推估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或
A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採 用：  
台積電預計 2030 年 開 始 導入 High NA  EUV ，比 12 吋 光 罩 微 影 系 統 預 計 全
面就緒的 2033 年 更 早 。 這 代 表 台 積 電 初 期 導 入High NA 時 ， 仍 會 先 使 用 現
行 6 吋 光 罩 ， 之 後 再 隨 12 吋 光 罩 與 相 關 系 統 成 熟 逐 步 轉 換 。

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

2 群益投顧 
根 據 台 積 電 目 前 公 開 的 製 程 藍 圖 ，A13 與 A12 製 程 都 預 計 2029 年 量 產 。 其
中 A13 是 A14 平 台 的 延 伸 ，A12 則 將 超 級 電 軌(Super Power Rail) 背面供電
技術導入 A14 平 台 。 因 此 N2 、 A16 、 A14 、 A13 與 A12 都 早 於 台 積 電 預 計
2030 年 導 入 High NA ， 推 估 High NA 將 會 在 A10 或 A10 以 下 的 先 進 製 程 採
用。  
Intel (INTC US) 和 Samsung (005930 KS) 領 先 台 積 電 採 用 High NA EUV ，但
也 同 時 參 與 12 吋 光 罩 開 發 ：  
Intel 在 2024 年 宣 布 完 成 全 球 首 套 商 用 High NA EUV 設 備 的 組 裝 ；2026 年
High NA 已 經 正 式 進 入 量 產 ， 並 用 於 部 分 18A 製 程 層 。 是 目 前 High NA 導
入 進 度 最 快 的 晶 片 製 造 商 。Samsung 則預計 2028 年導入 DRAM 量 產 ； 台 積
電選擇在 2030 年 導 入 。Intel 和 Samsung 也 同 時 表 示 與 ASML 及 供 應 鏈 合
作 ， 推 動 未 來 轉 向 大 尺 寸 光 罩 。 
台 積 電 與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，將 領 導 業
界 規 格 ：  
6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋光罩 的 這 項 升 級 非 單 純 的 尺 寸 放 大 ， 而 是 面 臨 生 態 系
與 物 理 極 限 的 多 重 挑 戰 。 整 體 供 應 鏈 重 構 ， 包 括 基 板 與 材 料 以 及 設 備 等 等 。
12 吋 光 罩 石 英 基 板 的 平 坦 度 、 缺 陷 密 度 與 熱 膨 脹 係 數 控 制 難 度 倍 增 。 製 程
設 備 如 薄 膜 沉 積、蝕 刻、清 洗 與 自 動 化 搬 運 設 備 皆 需 重 新 開 發。後 續 檢 測 與
量測極限檢測難度也增加，更大面積的圖形缺陷檢測（ Inspection ）與修補
技 術 需 要 突 破 。 在 12 吋 面 積 上 維 持 奈 米 級 的 對 位 精 度 與 線 寬 均 勻 性 極 為 困
難   
電 子 設 計 自 動 化 (EDA) 工 具 必 須 重 新 適 應 大 尺 寸 曝 光 視 場 與 資 料 處 理 。 12 
吋 光 罩 的 圖 形 檔 案 與 運 算 複 雜 度 將 大 幅 上 升 。 
台積電與 ASML 共 同 發 起 6 吋 光 罩 轉 移 至 12 吋 光 罩 的 規 格 轉 移，其 戰略意
義在於 ，台 積 電 為 全 球 最 大 晶 圓 代 工 廠，為 先 進 製 程 最 大 的 供 應 商，可 以 領
導 光 罩 基 底 供 應 商 、 光 罩 檢 測 設 備 商， 甚 至 EDA 廠 等 整 個 生 態 系 ，除 共同
分攤 ， 跨 越 升 級 12 吋 光 罩 的 龐 大 研 發 成 本外 ， 製 訂 規 格 和 標 準 ， 確 保 台 積
電 技 術 領 先 地 位 。因 此 對 台 積 電 的投 資 評 等 Buy ， 目 標 價 3,200 元。

相 關 個 股 獲 利 預 估 、 評 價 及 投 資 建 議

### Page 3

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 9 日

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

將反應 26 年 6 月 價 格 再 次 調 漲 續 增。

萬 張 上 修 至2027 年底達 930 萬 張 、2028 年 底 月 產 能 達 到1,110
萬張。 2027 年， NVIDIA  Rubin 平 台 將 帶 動CCL M8+ 、M9 材料
出貨攀升， AI ASIC CCL 拉 貨 動 能 呈 現50~60% 高 度 成 長 。

箔 基 板 升 級 至M8 等 級 推 升 材 料 成 本 與PCB 產 值 。

一. 2Q26 營收與財報 符合市場預期， 3Q26 展望正向  
2Q26 台 光 電 營 收472.75 億 元 ，+42.97%QoQ 、+110.03%YoY ， 毛 利
率為 33.85% ， 稅 後 淨 利98.73 億 元 ， 稅 後EPS+27.55 元。 2Q26 稼
動 率 維 持 滿 載 ， 受 惠AI 伺 服 器 需 求 帶 動 ，CCL M7 以 上 產 品 高 階
品 項 營 收 比 重 提 升 至60-65% ， 預 計 2H26 CCL 漲 價 持 續 帶 動 營 運
獲利增長。 3Q26 與 2H26 展望，台光電 7 月 營 收(192.06 億元 )、8
月營收 (201.45 億元 )，3Q26 累 計 達 成 率 約69%，符 合 市 場 預 期。由
於 2026 年 6 月 發 布 之 漲 價 效 應 可 反 應 在3Q26 毛 利 率 ， 預 估 台 光
電 3Q26 營收為 573.32 億 元，+21.27%QoQ ，+127.99%YoY ，毛 利 率
35.08% ， 稅 後 淨 利132.02 億 元 ， 單 季 稅 後EPS+36.85 元。

二. AI 伺服器帶動 CCL/PCB 產值提升，產能擴充 60% 
宏遠投顧觀察 ，AI 伺 服 器 與 先 進 封 裝 等 需 求 日 增，高 階CCL 市場
需求將在 2024 年~2026 年 CAGR 達 26%， 而 高 階CCL 供 給 端 CAGR
則是 7% ， 高 階 市 場 需 求 殷 切 。 高 階 銅 箔 基 板(包含 AI 伺服器和高
階 網 路 交 換 器 ， 自2024 年始採用 M8 CCL 材料； 2026 年 M8 材料
佔總體 CCL 市場 40% ，M8 材 料 佔 比 顯 著 高 於2022 年、 2023 年的
22% 和 26% 。 台 系 銅 箔 基 板 供 應 鏈 當 中 ，M6~M8 等 高 階 材 料 的 供
應廠商為 (2383) 台 光 電。回顧 近年來 台 光 電 產 能 爬 坡 速 度，2025 年
月產能 400~550 萬張、 2026 年 月 產 能615 萬 張 、 預 計2027 年 月 產
能從 700~800 萬張至 4Q27 增至 930 萬 張 、 預 計2028 年 底 月 產 能
達到 1,110 萬 張 ， 連 續4 年 年 產 能 擴 充 近60%。2027 年隨著 NVIDIA  
Rubin 平 台 將 帶 動CCL M8+ 、M9 材 料 出 貨 攀 升 ，AI ASIC CCL 拉

王淑君 
Jenny.wang@ctbcsis.com 
◎研究分析標的公司為本公司關係企業 
之客戶。

盟立 (2464 TT)

重新覆蓋  
買進(Buy) 
產能擴張推升 2027 成長，機器人接棒第二曲線  
⚫ 在手訂單充沛， 2027 營收成長能見度高  
⚫ 半導體需求強勁 ，產能同步擴張  
⚫ 機器人由小量走向量產，長期成長空間可期  
在手訂單充沛， 2027 營收成長能見度高 ：盟立 2025 年營收 66.81 億
元，毛利率 18.8% ，EPS -0.8 元，近年持續投入新客戶 與 新 專 案 開 發，
為後續擴產需求預作布局。進入 2026 年後營運逐步轉強， 2Q26 營收
21.69 億元 (QoQ +16% 、YoY +7.9%)， 毛利率 22.2%，EPS 0.42 元，1H26
轉虧為盈，主要受惠先進封裝擴產，帶動 OHT、AGV 及 Stocker 等產
品成長。截至 8 月累計接單約 140 億元，其中半導體占 75% 、智能物
流 10%~15% ，年底訂單有望接近 200 億元，且 75%~80% 可望於 2027
年底前完成， 預期今年營收呈現逐月、逐季提升趨勢 。 
半導體需求強勁，產能同步擴張 ：半導體仍是盟立未來最重要的成長
核心，公司目前聚焦先進封裝、高階載板及半導體自動化三大領域，
部分客戶擴產規劃已延伸至 2028~2029 年，部分載板客戶既有擴產仍
不足以滿足需求，持續尋找新廠房與土地。此外， 公司產品技術已具
備進入 Fab 的條件 ，若後續能成功由 OSAT 及先進封裝延伸至前段
Fab ，單一客戶需求量與設備價值將進一步放大。 產品面， 2027 年底
OHT 產能目標 2,500~3,000 台，對應月產能約 250 台，較先前月產能
100 台大幅成長 ；EFEM 產能目標 3,000 台，Stocker 500~800 台，AMR 
800~1,000 台， 整體 產能 規模 將明顯 放大 。 海外 市場 也是為 下一 階段
成長動能，目前有 9.3 億元訂單於海外執行。  
機器人由小量走向量產，長期成長空間可期 ：機器人方面，盟英進度
持續 推進， 目前北美客戶已開始小量出貨，年底諧波減速機產能目標
30 萬顆以上， 2027 年有望進一步提升至 80 萬顆。現階段仍處於量產
初期，固定成本、折舊與設備投入較高，產量需達到數十萬顆以上規
模才較容易跨過損平點。機器人 2026 年仍偏向小量貢獻， 2027 年開
始觀察損平與放量進度， 2028 年後 將成為第二成長曲線。 我們預估 盟
立 2026/2027 年營收達 98.5 /146.7 億元、EPS  2.51 /6.8 元，給予 「買進」
評等，目標價 238 元(2027 年 EPSx 35 倍)。 
風險因子： 半導體客戶擴產遞延 、機器人產品競爭加劇 。

### Page 2

個股報告

2 0 2 6 / 09 / 17

2

盟立成立於 1989 年，為台灣主要自動化 系統整合廠商之一，營運模式以「系
統整合 (SI)+ 自 有設 備 」為 核 心 ， 業務 涵 蓋半 導體 、 面 板 、物 流 及機 器人 等 領
域。近年公司策略明顯聚焦半導體自動化市場，主要產品包括空中走行式搬
送系統 (OHT) 、晶圓 /載 板 傳 送 設 備(EFEM) 、 晶 圓 儲 存 設 備(Stocker) 及 無 人 搬
運車 (AMR/AGV) 。 
生產據點方面，公司目前於竹科、銅鑼、中科及南科均設有生產基地，其中
竹科以機器人相關產品為主，銅鑼及南科負責 OHT ，中科則生產 Stocker 及
EFEM 。隨半導體客戶持續擴廠，公司近年積極進行產品規格標準化及生產流
程優化，包括 OHT 機種收斂、軌道規格統一，以及軟體平台模組化，有利後
續由專案型生產逐步轉向標準化、規模化製造，降低單位生產成本並提升交
付效率。我們認為，盟立在半導體自動化市場的核心競爭優勢，在於產品線
完整及軟硬體整合能力 。半導體廠內 OHT、EFEM、Stocker 及 AMR 之間需高
度整合，設備導入後具一定系統黏著度，其中 OHT、EFEM 與 AMR 等設備在
資訊串聯及搬運指令整合上具有較高連動性，有利盟立由單一設備進一步擴
大整體專案供應範圍。  
除半導體設備外，公司也積極布局機器人產業。旗下盟英科技主要投入諧波
減速機及關節模組，並透過 傲威科技向整機機器人領域延伸。 2026 年公司與
精確、敏實集團合資成立傲威科技，由盟立提供技術與產品、敏實集團負責
結構件，進一步強化機器人整機整合能力。  
圖表一、盟立產能配置

資料來源：盟立，中信投顧整理  
AI 伺服器、 HPC 及先進封裝需求持續成長，使全球半導體資本支出進入新一
輪擴張週期。本輪擴產範圍涵蓋晶圓代工、記憶體、先進封裝、封測及載板
等領域，同步帶動廠內自動化搬運設備需求。  
台積電 ，2026 年 Capex 已上修至 600 ～640 億美元 ，公司更宣布額外投資 1,000
億美元用以在美新建 4 座新廠 ；也規劃台灣未來數年將興建高達 13 座先進製
程及先進封裝廠房 。我們預估台積電 2026/2027/2028 年全球合計在建案量分
別達 16/16/5 座， 2026~2028 年 Capex 合計突破 2,000 億美元。

圖表二、 台積電各廠區興建時程表

封測產業進入擴產週期，受惠 AI 需求強勁及台積電先進封裝產能快速擴張，
部分後段封裝製程委外至 OSAT 廠，日月光、京元電、力成、矽格及南茂等
業者擴產規劃積極 。 對盟立而言 ， 封測與先進封裝產能擴張將同步帶動 OHT 、
Stocker 、AMR 等自動化搬運設備需求。  
目前盟立 OHT 於 OSAT 端已完成部分客戶認證並進入接單及專案執行階段；
前端 FAB 市場則持續推進驗證與導入 。 Stocker 已通過晶圓廠客戶驗證 ， EFEM
則受惠高階 ABF 載板客戶擴產需求，相關載板客戶建廠規劃已延伸至 2029
年。  
圖表 三、OSAT 廠擴廠進度整理

資料來源：各公司，中信投顧整理  
人形機器人目前仍處於由技術驗證走向量產的初期階段。人形機器人市場規
模 2025~2030 年 CAGR  47.1% ，2030 年市場規模估達 390 億美元，顯示中長
期產業具高度成長潛力。在人形機器人關鍵零組件中，諧波減速機主要應用
於機器人關節系統。單台人形機器人使用約 15~18 顆諧波減速機；新一代機
種可能增加至 20 餘顆。

## Document 053: 2548華固 第一金

### Page 1

楊凱云 
(886-2)2563-1666 
pineapple.yang@firstsec.com.tw

華固(2548) 
買進, TP 111 (潛在報酬 18.59%)  
買進 
Price(09/17):93.6 
Target Price：111

展望 2026年，完工案量與前一年相當，憑藉優異銷售策略與品牌
聲量，住宅案已全數完銷，商辦則持續與潛在買方洽談中，若期限
內未能找到整棟購置客戶，將改採分層銷售模式，預計年底前去化
約 50%，後續若順利完成整棟出售，則不排除上修獲利預估。展
望後市，公司持續獵地推案，目前建案能見度至 2034年，銷售中
總銷逾千億元，待推出總銷逾 900 億元，顯示公司中長期業績成
長無虞，惟短期受房市景氣及銷售速度放緩影響，業績表現仍存在
不確定性。股利方面，公司過往現金股利最低配發5元，且現金股
利配發率長期維持 70~80%，推估 2027 年現金股利有望達 6~7
元。綜合以上，研究部預估 2026、2027年 EPS 7.28、9.78元，
以 1.4*PBR評價，給予目標價 111元，投資建議為買進。 
 近期投資評等  營運概況 
1H26 營收 57.07億元，YoY+181.4%，稅後損益 9.43 億元，
YoY+307%，EPS 2.92 元，略低於研究部預期。上半年營收
主要來自新案「華固上文林」持續交屋挹注，全案總銷 61 億
元，已順利於8M26 完成過戶。獲利方面，1H26 毛利率 27%，
YoY-5.8ppt，由於新推案土地成本較高，加上興建期間物料及
工資上漲，導致利潤率略低於預期。

 公司展望 
展望 2026 年 ， 全年將迎來三大建案完工交屋 ， 合計總銷為179
億元，YoY-2.8%，分別為「華固一莊」(總銷 41 億元，完銷，
8M26 交屋)、「華固上文林」(總銷 61 億元，完銷，1H26交
屋)、「華固時代置地」(總銷 77 億元，持續與潛在客戶洽談
整棟出售，若期限內未能找到整棟購置客戶，公司將改採分層
銷售模式，預計年底前去化約 50%)。獲利方面，住宅案因土
地成本墊高，個案毛利率應介於25~26%之間；商辦產品則因
市場降溫，成交價格與利潤率回歸合理水準，毛利率預估約
28~30%，使全年合併毛利率將較 2025 年下滑，預估為
26.8%，YoY-1.43ppt。綜合以上，研究部預估 2026 年營收
140.85 億元， YoY-22.77%，稅後損益 23.27 億元，
Yo-28.21%，EPS 7.28元。

展望 2027 年，預計有三大建案完工交屋，合計總銷為205 億

 元，YoY+14.5%，分別為「華固頂匯」(總銷 108 億元，銷售

成完銷仍有一定難度，加上兩案完工時程接近年底，實際入帳
時程仍具不確定性，故預估整體入帳率約 60%，若加計「華
固時代置地」順利完銷，全年營收有望挑戰 160 億元大關。
綜合以上，研究部預估 2027 年營收 166.3 億元，
YoY+18.06%，稅後損益31.28 億元，YoY+34.43%，EPS 9.78
元。

 推案計畫及銷售中個案概況 
銷售中個案： 「華固頂匯」總銷108 億元，銷售率70~80%(僅
開放 200 戶銷售，剩餘 60 戶預計採成屋銷售)；「華固豐匯」
總銷 47 億元，銷售率 35~40%(出現少量退戶)；「華固慕川」
總銷 50 億元，銷售率95~100%； 「華固織幸」總銷33 億元，
銷售率 95~100%； 「吾雙」總銷67 億元，銷售率95~100%、
「華固芙心」 總銷53 億元，銷售率95~100%、 「華固四季匯」
總銷 54 億元，銷售率35~40%、「華固譽誠」總銷54 億元，
銷售率 85~90%、「華固創匯園區」總銷 325 億元，銷售率
25~30%。

2027年公司將再推出 「央北二案」(總銷64億元 ， 單價105~110
萬/坪，總價 4~5 千萬)、「松勇路案」(總銷 70億元，250 萬
/坪，2~3 億/戶，合計總銷為 134 億元，預計於 2030~2031
年完工交屋。

市況方面，2H24 起國內房市受限貸令及信用管制措施影響，
台中地區產品銷售速度趨緩，雙北地區則相對穩健，因此公司
未來獵地方向將回歸雙北，並以 60%住宅+40%廠辦為目標。
隨房市移轉量及房價已明顯降溫，因此央行近期放寬第二戶貸
款成數至 7 成，有助於提振換屋需求及購屋信心，最壞情況已
過，整體銷售動能有望回溫。

土地庫存方面，尚有逾 900 億元待推案將陸續於 2027~2030
年推出，並於 2034 年前完工挹注，顯示公司中長期業績成長

預計年底前去化約 50%，後續若順利完成整棟出售，則不排
除上修獲利預估。展望後市，公司持續獵地推案，目前建案能
見度至 2034 年，銷售中總銷逾千億元，待推出總銷逾900 億
元，顯示公司中長期業績成長無虞，惟短期受房市景氣及銷售
速度放緩影響，業績表現仍存在不確定性。股利方面，公司過
往現金股利最低配發 5 元，且現金股利配發率長期維持
70~80%，推估 2027年現金股利有望達 6~7 元。綜合以上，
研究部預估 2026、2027年 EPS 7.28、9.78元，以 1.4*PBR
評價，給予目標價 111 元，投資建議為買進。

## Document 067: 260915_ms_MLCC

Source PDF: all report pdf/260915_ms_MLCC.pdf

Pages: 43

### Page 2

Japan Insight
2
Investment Perspective
MLCC is early cycle in AI server & rack penetration
There is immense change underway for the MLCC industry with an investment narrative
that is changing as AI chips/server demand is increasingly driving MLCC scarcity.  AI is
shifting from semiconductors to MLCCs, creating a supply/demand mismatch in high-end
components. The high-end AI MLCC market is effectively a Japanese-Korean duopoly with
Murata and SEMCO together hold around 85% share. High-capacitance, low-ESL parts for

AI accelerators require hundreds to over a thousand dielectric layers with near-zero
defects and face strict customer qualification — the barrier preventing a repeat of 2017-
18, when shortages invited entry and the cycle self-corrected. AI platforms now compete
directly with consumer, automotive, and industrial applications for advanced materials,
heightening supply risk for MLCC traditional customers. We are still in the early innings,
and supply concerns are extending beyond near-term shortages to longer-term supply

security. As a result, we are seeing an increasing number of long-term supply agreements
being signed between suppliers and customers.
Exhibit 1: Sequencing the technology cycle – still early cycle for MLCC
Source:  Morgan Stanley Research estimates
Turning point in MLCC pricing
While Murata Mfg.’s CFO and Executive Deputy President Masanori Minamide stated in a
Sep 14 Nikkei interview that rising production loads for high-end products are making it

increasingly difficult to fully supply low-end products, and that the priority of defending
low-end market share has effectively declined, we believe the company’s fundamental
policy of not changing prices based solely on short-term supply-demand conditions
remains intact. That said, in our view, Murata has been gradually shifting its strategy
toward focusing more on its areas of strength and adopting pricing that better reflects the
need to cover large-scale capital investments, particularly in those core segments where it

enjoys a competitive advantage. We believe that Murata’s reluctance to enter into L TAs for
AI/data-center use value added MLCCs reflects its view that locking in prices today may
not be optimal, given the potential for MLCC pricing to rise in the future.
We expect sales growth to continue for small-sized, high-
capacity, high-value-added MLCCs for AI servers/DCs

### Page 3

M
Japan Insight
Morgan Stanley MUFG 3
MLCCs are a key business, accounting for 50% of sales at Murata, 46% at SEMCO, 19% at
Yageo, and 70% at Taiyo Yuden in 2025. We expect global MLCC shipment value to
expand at a CAGR of 20.3%, from $18.51bn in 2025 to $44.45bn in 2031. We believe
growth is set to accelerate from the 5.0% CAGR recorded in global MLCC shipment value
over the 13 years of 2012-25 because we expect continued growth in sales of small, high-

capacity, high-value-added MLCCs for AI servers/datacenters (DCs) from 2026. For AI
servers/DCs alone, we forecast MLCC shipment value to reach $23.33bn by 2031 (vs. our
previous estimate of $10.80bn), implying a 57.1% CAGR (vs. 38.1%).
Howard Kao (our Greater China Technology Hardware analyst) expects MLCC demand
volume for VR200 NVL72 racks to increase about 80% versus GB300 NVL72 racks, and
forecasts the number of MLCCs mounted in each Rubin NVL72 rack will jump to roughly

570,000 units from around 320,000 for GB300 NVL72 racks, with MLCC dollar content
per rack estimated to increase by 166%.　We expect the rapid expansion of the high-value-
added MLCC market for AI servers/DCs to be especially positive for  earnings at Murata (#1
market share in MLCCs for 2025 at 41%) and SEMCO (#2 at 23%).

Key roles of MLCCs in AI/DC
MLCCs play a role in locally stabilizing power supply (instantaneous current supply plus

noise removal) near semiconductors, such as GPUs, CPUs, and FPGAs. Since GPUs, CPUs,
and FPGAs repeatedly switch on and off on a nanosecond timescale, they demand large
currents instantaneously, and MLCCs meet this instantaneous current demand by
immediately discharging their stored charge. As semiconductor miniaturization advances,
circuit operating speed increases and drive voltage falls. As voltage decreases, even small
voltage fluctuations risk causing semiconductor malfunction, so even more stable power

supply voltage is required than before. Demand for capacitor capacitance is also increasing
in line with AI servers' rising computing power and power consumption, and because
board area and mountable space are limited, we expect demand for smaller, higher-
capacity MLCCs to persist.
Sharp dips vs. share price peaks provide re-entry opportunity
Year-to-date to Sep 11, share prices of the four companies had risen 127% for Murata,
449% for SEMCO, 141% for Yageo, and 160% for Taiyo Yuden. However, from their

respective June-July peaks, these have fallen 40% for Murata, 38% for SEMCO, 52% for

### Page 4

M
Japan Insight
4
Yageo, and 59% for Taiyo Yuden. Considering the strong likelihood that high-value-added
products for AI servers/DCs will continue to drive growth in MLCC demand not only in
2026 but also from 2027 onward, and that each company's earnings will stay on an
expansionary trend, we believe the conditions are now in place to consider re-entering
these stocks.

Earnings and share price risks
We expect capital expenditure on AI/DC to keep expanding and generational transitions to

### Page 6

M
Japan Insight
6
continue in GPUs, sustaining growth in demand for small, high-capacity, high-value-added
MLCCs. Risks to the continuity of AI/DC capex expansion include: (1) rising risk from the
growing practice of hyperscalers and major semiconductor companies charging usage fees
in exchange for equity investments in AI development companies, conditional on the use
of their own cloud services or semiconductors; and (2) risk that AI/DC capex growth slows

due to bottlenecks, such as power infrastructure constraints. If the pace of AI/DC capex
growth slows, we cannot rule out that MLCC demand falls short of our base case outlook
for demand. Also, as shown in Exhibits Exhibit 8 Exhibit 8  and Exhibit 9 , during the IT
bubble around 2000, there was a strong correlation between the YoY change in Murata's
and Taiyo Yuden's monthly order intake and their market cap. Since order intake has risen
sharply since Jul-Sep 2025, we cannot ignore the possibility that YoY growth in order

intake will slow. On the other hand, MLCC demand continues to expand for high-value-
added products for AI/DC, and we expect the major MLCC makers' sales to grow along
with an improving product mix.

High-value-added products for AI servers will likely drive
MLCC market growth
We expect global MLCC shipment value to increase at 20.3%
CAGR  through 2031
As shown in Exhibit 10 ,  we estimate that global MLCC shipment value fell for two
consecutive years, from $17.47bn in 2021 (up 26.0% YoY) to $14.32bn in 2022 (down
18.1%) and $12.64bn in 2023 (down 11.7%), before rising back up to $13.30bn in 2024 (up
5.2%) and $14.67bn in 2025 (up 10.3%). We increase our forecasts for shipment value from

$18.48bn to $18.51bn for 2026 (up 26.2% YoY), from $22.16bn to $24.52bn for 2027 (up
32.4%), and from $25.73bn to $28.60bn for 2028 (up 16.7%), and we expect the market to
reach $44.45bn in 2031, growing at a CAGR of 20.3% over the six-year period.

AI/DC MLCC market to continue growing at a rapid pace
We expect growth in demand to accelerate for high-value-added products for AI/DC from

the second half of 2026. Howard Kao  expects MLCC demand volume for VR200 NVL72
racks to increase about 80% versus GB300 NVL72 racks, and forecasts the number of
MLCCs mounted in each Rubin NVL72 rack will rise sharply to roughly 570,000 units from
around 320,000 units for GB300 NVL72 racks (Exhibit 11 ). This increase stems from a
rise in the number of MLCCs mounted on both compute boards and switch boards, as well
as an increase in PCB content across the overall system, including modules such as CX9

Orchid. Howard Kao estimates this increase in MLCC mounting volume will lift the dollar-
based MLCC content per rack by 166% ( Exhibit 12 ).

Role of MLCCs in AI servers
Capacitors, including multi-layer ceramic capacitors (MLCCs), function as a “dam” that
temporarily stores electricity, and are an essential electronic component for noise removal

and voltage stabilization in virtually all electronic circuits, including those used in
computers, smartphones, and automobiles. MLCCs play a role in locally stabilizing power
supply (instantaneous current supply plus noise removal) near semiconductors such as
GPUs, CPUs, and FPGAs. Since GPUs, CPUs, and FPGAs repeatedly switch on and off on a
nanosecond timescale, they demand large currents instantaneously, and MLCCs meet this
instantaneous current demand by immediately discharging their stored charge. At the

same time, MLCCs bypass only the AC component (noise and fluctuating current), which
also serves to prevent noise from entering GPUs, CPUs, and FPGAs. MLCCs feature low
ESR (low resistance) and low ESL (low inductance), allowing them to track even

### Page 10

M
Japan Insight
10
nanosecond-level current fluctuations in semiconductors such as GPUs, CPUs, and FPGAs. 
MLCCs are often placed around components such as GPUs and ASICs that operate at low 
voltages of around 1.0V. As semiconductor miniaturization advances, circuit operating 
speed increases and drive voltage falls. As voltage decreases, even small voltage 
fluctuations risk causing semiconductor malfunction, requiring an even more stable power 
supply voltage than before. Demand for capacitor capacitance will also rise along with AI 
servers' improving computing power and increasing power consumption, and because 
board area and mountable space are limited, demand for smaller, higher-capacity MLCCs 
will likely continue. 
MLCC miniaturization and higher capacity to accelerate with 
VR200 NVL72
We assume that the space available for mounting MLCCs on VR200 NVL72 will not 
change significantly from the GB300 NVL72 rack, and that the transition from GB300 to 
VR200 will require installing higher-capacity MLCCs within the limited space of 
semiconductor ABF package substrates and AI accelerator boards, accelerating growth in 
demand for small-sized, high-capacity MLCCs. Murata has consistently been a leader in 
MLCC miniaturization and capacity increases, beginning mass production of 1005-size 1 F 
MLCCs in 2000 and 0402-size 1 F and 1005-size 22 F MLCCs in 2020. We expect 
demand for 1608-size 100 F, 1005-size 47 F, and 0603-size 10 F MLCCs to increase 
substantially with VR200 NVL72. We estimate that 1608-size 100 F MLCCs command a 
unit price 5-10x higher than 2012-size 100 F or 1608-size 47 F MLCCs, and we expect 
the shift to smaller, higher-capacity MLCCs accompanying the transition to VR200 NVL72 
to drive a substantial rise in ASP.

High-capacitance MLCCs (47µF+) are likely to capture a disproportionate share of this 
incremental opportunity: Our analysis suggests that 47µF+ MLCCs could account for 
approximately 50-60% of the incremental Cloud AI MLCC TAM in CY27, representing an 
additional ~US$2B market opportunity alone. This reflects both the increasing use of high-

### Page 11

M
Japan Insight
Morgan Stanley MUFG 11
capacitance devices within AI accelerator systems and a growing mix shift toward higher-
value MLCC content.
From a volume perspective, we estimate Cloud AI demand for 47µF+ MLCCs could 
increase from approximately 4 billion units in CY25 to 40B+ units by CY27: Such a 
rapid increase would represent a meaningful step-up in industry demand and could require 
substantial capacity reallocation or expansion among leading suppliers.
Based on our industry discussions, the market for high-capacitance MLCCs remains 
concentrated among a limited number of suppliers, primarily Japanese and Korean 
manufacturers, including Murata, Taiyo Yuden, and Samsung Electro-Mechanics (SEMCO). 
As demand from AI infrastructure accelerates, we believe capacity utilization for these 
products could tighten, raising the possibility of capacity crowding-out effects across 
other end markets and applications.

Materials development and production technology behind
MLCC miniaturization and capacity increases
In MLCCs, smaller sizes and higher capacities have mainly been achieved by thinning the
dielectric and electrode layers (layer thinning) and increasing the number of layers
stacked (multilayering). For high-capacity products for AI/DC, in addition to the layer
thinning and multilayering described above, efforts are accelerating to further miniaturize

and increase capacity by expanding the area of the dielectric and internal electrodes per
dielectric sheet. We believe MLCC miniaturization and capacity increases depend on: (1)
developing superior dielectric and electrode materials; (2) manufacturing highly uniform,
highly reliable slurry; (3) technology for precisely　stacking dielectric sheets; and (4)
technology for firing in a controlled atmosphere without oxidation. For high-capacity
products for AI/DC, we see the key factors as (1) materials development that increases the

crystallinity of dielectric materials while making them finer, in order to thin each dielectric
layer as much as possible (layer thinning), together with (2) manufacturing technology to
precisely stack the dielectric sheets.

Dielectric material manufacturing process
Dielectric materials are manufactured by adding a binder, solvent, and additives to barium
titanate (BaTiO3), which is produced from barium carbonate (B aCO3) and titanium oxide

(TiO2). Methods for manufacturing barium titanate are broadly divided into the solid-
phase method and the liquid-phase method. In the solid-phase method, barium carbonate
and titanium oxide are mixed and fired at high temperature, producing barium titanate
through a chemical reaction at the firing stage. In the liquid-phase method, barium
carbonate and titanium oxide are dissolved in a solution, reacted at the solution stage, and
fired at a relatively low temperature to produce the compound. Manufacturing methods

used in mass production via the liquid-phase method include the oxalate method and the
hydrothermal synthesis method. Barium titanate with small and uniform particle size is
key to smaller and higher-capacity MLCCs. In 2023, Murata established MF Materials, a
joint venture with Ishihara Sangyo and Ishihara Sangyo's wholly owned subsidiary Fuji
Titanium (with equity stakes of 20% for Murata, 10% for Ishihara Sangyo, and 70% for Fuji
Titanium). MF Materials produces barium titanate via the oxalate process and supplies it

to Murata Manufacturing.　MF Materials is constructing a new building adjacent to its head
office plant in Nobeoka, Miyazaki Prefecture, with operations scheduled to commence in
2027. TDK established a joint venture with Nippon Chemical Industrial in April 2026 called
TDK-NCI Advanced Materials (with equity stakes of 51% for TDK and 49% for Nippon
Chemical Industrial), with plans to accelerate development of the oxalate method barium
titanate.

Murata and SEMCO to benefit the most from growing
demand for high-value-added products for AI servers
We expect Murata and SEMCO, which supply small, high-capacity products stably and in
large volumes, to capture the greatest benefits from growing demand for small-sized,
high-capacity MLCCs used in AI servers and data centers. Murata has explained that AI/DC

MLCC sales accounted for 10-15% of its MLCC sales in F3/26 and are forecast to rise 100-
105% YoY in F3/27, accounting for 20-25% of MLCC sales. We estimate Murata's AI/DC
MLCC sales to rise roughly 120% YoY in F3/27 and about 100% YoY in F3/28, with AI/DC
MLCCs underpinning growth in the company's overall MLCC sales. Murata has stated that
it forecasts a 100-105% increase in AI/DC MLCC sales in F3/27, reflecting a roughly 40%
increase in volume and a roughly 50% rise in ASP, but we expect the increase in ASP to

exceed its forecast.
2025 MLCC market share was Murata 40.8%, Taiyo Yuden
11.3%
We estimate that global MLCC shipment value totaled $14.67bn in 2025 (up 10.3% YoY),
with shares of 40.8% for Murata, 22.5% for SEMCO, 11.3% for Taiyo Yuden, 6.9% for TDK,
and 5.4% for Yageo, and a combined 13.0% for all companies outside these top five. We
expect demand for high-value-added products for AI/DC to keep expanding from 2027,
with Murata and SEMCO continuing to gain market share.

What are the risks to Murata?
Risks for Murata include: (1) losing MLCC market share by failing to adequately supply
MLCCs for AI servers/DCs amid rapidly expanding demand. (2) Murata's products,
including MLCCs, are used not only in AI servers/DCs but across virtually all electronic
equipment, including automobiles, smartphones, and industrial machinery, making the
company sensitive to macroeconomic conditions and inventory cycles. (3) The company

has explained that a ¥1/$ appreciation of the yen would reduce operating profit by ¥4.5bn
per year, and the yen could appreciate beyond our assumed rate of ¥155/$, among other
factors.

Murata Manufacturing earnings forecasts
We expect Murata at 2Q results (end-Oct) to hike F3/27
guidance; continued earnings growth from F3/28
F3/27-28 and F3/27 2Q forecasts: We project F3/27 OP of ¥490.8bn (guidance ¥430bn,
FactSet consensus ¥453.9bn), up ¥208.9bn YoY and above F3/22's record of ¥424.1bn.
That is because we expect profit in the Components segment to rise ¥186.2bn to ¥501.3bn
and losses in the Device & Modules segment to shrink by ¥24.1bn from -¥26.5bn to -

¥2.4bn. We look for Components profit to rise due to a +31.6% YoY increase in MLCC
sales in dollar terms (+36.1% on yen basis), a continued increase after F3/26’s +13.8% rise
(+12.6% on yen basis), product mix improvement, and higher utilization rates absorbing
increased depreciation and other fixed cost growth. We expect Device & Modules losses
to shrink mainly due to the falling out of ¥43.8bn in Resonant goodwill impairment and
¥6bn from the disposal of the micro battery business. We project F3/28 OP of ¥702.6bn

(consensus ¥655.3bn). The projected ¥211.8bn YoY growth breaks down as Components +
¥170bn and Devices & Modules +¥39.6bn. We expect Components profit to rise thanks to
a 24.5% YoY increase in capacitor (MLCC) sales in dollars (+23.7% on yen basis) and
continued product mix improvement absorbing increased depreciation & other fixed cost
growth. We look for Devices & Modules profit to rise on factors including increased sales
of high-frequency modules, secondary batteries, and vertical power delivery modules.

We project 2Q OP of ¥125bn (consensus ¥122.9bn). The projected QoQ ¥26.6bn growth
breaks down as Components +¥12.5bn QoQ to ¥126bn, and Devices & Modules +¥13.9bn
QoQ to ¥1bn. For Components, we expect sales of MLCCs for AI/data centers to continue
to increase, the MLCC product mix to improve, and utilization rates to continue to rise. For
Devices & Modules, we expect sales for North American smartphones to increase
seasonally and reenter the black, but because North American smartphone production is

split between fall models and spring models, profit contributions are likely to be limited.

Murata’s share price risk/reward: Even though we raise our earnings estimates for

Murata, we lower our DCF-based price target from ¥12,500 to ¥11,000, mainly reflecting
an increase in our DCF model’s risk-free rate from 2.60% to 3.0%. Murata's share price has
fallen 41% year-to-date from its June 22 peak, and we see 53% upside over the next 12-18
months, maintain our OW rating, and designate the stock as our Top Pick in Japan's
electronic components industry. The reasons are as follows: (1) We forecast the dollar-
based shipment value of MLCCs, where Murata held the top global share of 40% in 2025,

to expand at a CAGR of 21% through 2031, with Murata continuing to gradually gain
market share. (2) Demand for MLCCs used in AI servers/DCs is likely to continue
expanding, and demand for small, high-capacity, high-value-added products, which Murata
has brought to market ahead of competitors, should grow substantially. (3) Outside of
MLCCs, we look for demand to expand for small, high-value-added products, including
secondary batteries for DC BBUs, power modules for vertical power delivery in AI servers,

and RF modules for smartphones, and then expand even further as edge AI devices

become widespread. (4) In addition to earnings growth, we expect that management’s
proactive approach to shareholder returns, including share buybacks, will raise capital
efficiency and lift ROE.
Bull case ¥14,000, 95% projected upside
In our bull case, we assume higher sales of MLCCs for AI servers/data centers than in our
base case, for component sales of 7% higher than our base case, for OP of ¥560.3bn in
F3/27, ¥794bn in F3/28, and ¥1.515tn in F3/31. Our forex assumptions and DCF

assumptions are the same as for our base case. Bull case fair value corresponds to 58.2x of
fully-diluted F3/27 EPS of ¥240.4, 41.1x fully-diluted F3/28 EPS of ¥340.7, and 21.1x fully-
diluted F3/31 EPS of ¥664.0.
Bear case ¥3,500
Murata products are used in all kinds of different electronic devices, and they are easily
affected by the macro economy and inventory cycle. In our bear case, we assume slower
global economic growth rates and lower production of high-end consumer goods like

high-end smartphones than in our base case for OP of ¥314.6bn in F3/27, ¥330bn in F3/28,
and ¥362.4bn in F3/31. Our forex assumptions and DCF assumptions are the same as in
our base case. Here we look for ROE to increase from 8.8% in F3/26 to 9.3% in F3/27 and
9.4% in F3/28, above capital cost of 7.5%. With profit below the market consensus, we
assume the share price falls to a P/B of 2.4x.

SEMCO earnings forecasts
Shawn Kim
AI-driven MLCC growth and pricing to drive sharp earnings
acceleration in FY27, with continued growth in FY28
FY26-28 forecasts: We project SEMCO OP of W2.34tn in FY26 (+156% YoY), W5.70tn in
FY27 and W8.36tn in FY28, implying further growth of 144% and 47%, respectively. We
expect the earnings acceleration to be driven mainly by the Components (MLCC) and
Package Solution businesses, while the Optics business remains a relatively modest

contributor. In FY27, our projected OP of W5.70tn breaks down as Components W4.56tn,
Package Solution W943bn, and Optics W201bn, versus W1.67tn, W510bn, and W165bn,
respectively, in FY26. In FY28, we forecast Components OP rising further to W6.55tn,
Package Solution to W1.58tn, and Optics to W230bn. As such, we expect SEMCO's consol.
OP margin to grow from 8.1% in FY25 to 15.7% in FY26, 24.5% in FY27, and 25.9% in FY28.
We maintain the stock as our top pick within our S. Korea Technology coverage.

For Components, we expect profit growth to accelerate as AI-server demand drives both
strong MLCC volume growth and a structurally richer product mix. High-end AI MLCC
supply remains tight, with lead times exceeding 20 weeks, supply-chain inventories lean
following two years of destocking, and book-to-bill at the three high-end suppliers
reaching its highest level since the pandemic. Importantly, contract pricing has increasingly
been institutionalized through long-term agreements, while the high-end AI MLCC market

remains concentrated, with Murata and SEMCO together holding around 85% share.
Capacity additions are also unlikely to close the supply-demand gap quickly, as most
meaningful new capacity is scheduled for 2027 or later and suppliers are generally
targeting only around 10-15% annual output growth. We thus expect tighter supply-
demand, higher pricing, and a shift toward high-capacitance, low-ESL AI products to drive
Components OP margin from 21.4% in FY26 to 30.9% in FY27 and 30.1% in FY28.

Bull case W3,000,000
Our bull case assumes ABF opportunities from major US CSPs drive stronger content
growth, with future ASIC content & volume growth continuing to outpace our base-case
expectations. Customer diversification toward non-mobile customers should also reduce
earnings volatility and improve profitability. Our bull case, equivalent to 15x FY27e P/B,
reflects what we see as an unprecedented super-cycle in the underlying AI-related MLCC

and ABF markets.
Bear case W1,350,000
In our bear case, we assume slower content growth in MLCCs and substrates due to
greater competition and China's localization, together with prolonged consumer weakness
weighing on the Optics business. However, we still expect increasing AI exposure to
structurally lift SEMCO's MLCC and ABF ASPs, product mix and margins, given the
difficulty commodity suppliers face in penetrating the premium market. Our bear-case

value corresponds to 7x FY27e P/B. Key downside risks include a significant pullback in

### Page 22

M
Japan Insight
22
Samsung's mobile flagship cycle, lower SEMCO market share, execution risk in penetrating
Chinese smartphone customers, and continued consumer-demand weakness.

Morgan Stanley MUFG 25
Yageo earnings forecasts
Howard Kao
Tightening MLCC Narrative is Unchanged
We forecast CY26 revenue of approximately NT$183B, up 38% y/y, driven primarily by
stronger AI-related demand, with a more modest contribution from consumer
applications. This demand strength should support broad-based utilization improvement
across Yageo’s passive component portfolio. Pricing is also turning more favorable, with
distributor price increases implemented in 2Q26 and Yageo raising prices for direct

customers in 3Q26, as previously highlighted. The combination of higher utilization and
firmer pricing should drive meaningful operating leverage. We forecast gross margin
expanding to 39.3%, up 310bps y/y, and operating margin reaching 27.4%, up 500bps y/y,
supporting EPS of approximately NT$19.79.
Looking into CY27, we expect supply/demand conditions in the mid- and low-end markets
to tighten further, sustaining pricing momentum and driving an additional improvement in

utilization. We therefore forecast revenue of approximately NT$252B, up 38% y/y.
Continued operating leverage should lift gross margin to 43.2%, an increase of 390bps y/y,
and operating margin to 33.4%, up 600bps y/y, resulting in EPS of approximately NT$32.11.
Why has the stock been so volatile?
Yageo benefited from a sharp increase in MLCC pricing in 2Q26, driven by a temporary
spike in spot-market demand. In our view, ODM/EMS customers accelerated inventory

replenishment amid concerns that the ramp in high-end AI-server MLCC demand,
particularly alongside Nvidia’s Vera Rubin platform, could constrain supply of mid- and
low-end MLCCs. Under normal market conditions, these customers typically carry only 3-4
weeks of inventory. However, supply concerns prompted a rapid increase in inventory
coverage to approximately 4-6 months.
During this restocking phase, ODM/EMS customers sourced MLCCs from both
manufacturers, including Yageo, and distributors. The resulting demand surge drove a

sharp increase in spot/distributor pricing and also contributed to the stock’s strong
performance. However, the pricing move was largely driven by a one-off inventory build.
Once customers had secured sufficient inventory, purchasing activity normalized and spot
pricing began to soften. This, together with weaker broader market sentiment, led to a
pullback of ~50% from the stock’s peak of approximately NT$1,140.
At the current share price, however, we believe the risk-reward has become attractive. The

underlying fundamentals, and, importantly, our view of the MLCC upcycle, remain intact.
Our analysis indicates substantial incremental demand for high-end, high-capacitance
MLCCs of 47µF and above, which will require a meaningful increase in dedicated
production capacity. We expect this to drive further capacity conversion toward high-end
MLCCs, with the resulting crowding-out effect becoming more visible from late CY26 into
early CY27. This should progressively tighten supply in the mid- and low-end MLCC

market and provide renewed support for pricing.

Price Target Discussion and Valuation Methodology
We lower our 12-month price target to NT$1,050 (from NT$1,465): This reflects our
lower earnings estimates for CY26-28. Our price target is our base case value, derived
from our residual income valuation model. Similar to our analysis of other tech hardware
companies within our coverage, we think this methodology provides the most accurate

value of the firm by taking into account the cost of equity.
Our key assumptions are:
• Cost of equity of 7.8%:
° Risk-free rate of 1.0% (10-year Taiwanese government bond yield);
° Equity risk premium of 6.8%;
° Beta of 1.1;

### Page 27

M
Japan Insight
Morgan Stanley MUFG 27
• Medium-term growth rate of 12% (down from 16%);
• Terminal growth rate of 3%.
Our price target implies 33x 2027e P/E and 24x 2028e P/E. These are above its five-year
historical range of 8x to 18x, which we view as justified considering its longer-term
transformation story to become a global total solutions provider, moving from passive
into active components. In addition, compared to global passive component peers, we

believe Yageo stock remains undervalued with higher ROE and margins but lower P/E vs.
Japanese peers (which trade on an average at 40x+ P/E).
Our bull and bear case scenario values decrease by magnitudes similar to the change
to our base case value, to NT$1,490 and NT$480, respectively.

Taiyo Yuden earnings forecasts
We expect expect record profit in F3/29, not F3/28; upgrade
the stock from UW to EW
Full-term F3/27 and F3/28 earnings forecasts, F3/27 2Q forecasts: We project F3/27 OP
of ¥45.1bn (guidance ¥45bn, FactSet consensus ¥42bn), up +¥25.1bn YoY. We expect sales
in dollars of core product capacitors (MLCCs) to increase +22.6% YoY (+27.4% on yen
basis), a continued increase following on FY3/26’s +10.4% rise (+8.5% on yen basis), and

higher utilization rates absorbing increased depreciation expense and other fixed cost
increases. We project F3/28 OP of ¥66.1bn (consensus ¥73.1bn), up +¥25bn YoY. We look
for sales in dollars of capacitors (MLCCs) to rise +17.3% YoY (+16.6% on a yen basis),
absorbing increased depreciation expense and other fixed cost increases. We expect OP to
rise ¥83.8bn in F3/29 (consensus ¥107.1bn), surpassing the historical high profit of
¥68.2bn in F3/22.

We project 2Q OP of ¥12.6bn (consensus ¥11bn), up +¥7.8bn from ¥4.8bn in 1Q. We look
for MLCC sales to rise +18.1% compared with 1Q and for the utilization rate to rise from
85% in 1Q to 95% in 2Q. With increased sales of inductors for North American
smartphones, there should be a major profit increase on 1Q.

Taiyo Yuden share price risk/reward: We upgrade Taiyo Yuden from UW to EW, as we
believe its share price, having fallen 56.0% from its July 1 peak, has limited downside risk

and offers 6% upside over the next 12-18 months. We lower our DCF-based price target
from ¥12,500 to ¥10,500. This mainly reflects an increase in the DCF model’s risk-free
rate from 2.60% to 3.0%. We rate the stock EW rather than OW because: (1) In its core
MLCC business, we estimate Taiyo Yuden's MLCC market share was 11.3% in 2025, down
from 12.7% in 2020, and we think this downtrend continued into 2026, with its market
share shrinking to 10.7% in 1Q and 9.9% in 2Q. (2) We believe Taiyo Yuden is lagging

behind Murata (No. 1 share) and SEMCO (No. 2) in small, high-capacity MLCCs for AI
server accelerator boards, where demand is rapidly expanding. (3) We expect consensus
estimates for F3/28 onward to be revised down after 2Q results.
Bull case ¥14,000, 49% projected upside
In our bull case, F3/27 sales of MLCCs for AI servers/data centers are 7% higher than in our
base case, for OP of ¥56.5bn in F3/27, ¥85.5bn in F3/28, and ¥176.3bn in F3/31. Our forex

assumptions and DCF assumptions match our base case. Bull case fair value corresponds
to 50.7x our fully-diluted F3/27 EPS estimate of ¥276.1, 33.6x our fully-diluted F3/28 EPS
estimate of ¥417.0, and 15.9x our full-diluted F3/31 EPS estimate of ¥879.1.
Bear case ¥4,500
Taiyo Yuden’s care products (MLCCs and inductors) are used in all kinds of electronic
devices, and are easily affected by the macro economy and inventory cycle. In our bear
case, we assume slower global economic growth rates and lower production of high-end

consumer goods like high-end smartphones than in our base case, for OP of ¥39.2bn in
F3/27, ¥56.1bn in F3/28, and ¥65.8bn in F3/31. Our forex assumptions and DCF
assumptions are the same as in our base case. Here we look for ROE of 7.3% in F3/27 and
10.1% in F3/28, improving to a level above capital cost of 7.9%. With profit below market
expectations, we assume the share price falls to a P/B of 1.8x.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over
the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.

Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

ab 15 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
Powertech T echnology
2026 T aiwan Summit: FOPLP to see more
revenue contribution from 2027F
FOPLP business supported by AMD and Broadcom
PTI attended our T aiwan Summit on Sep 14th. Management noted its  FOPLP capacity in
T aiwan will be at 6K substrate/month (3K in 2026 and add another 3K in 2027 with full
ramp in 2028) with panel substrate size of 510 mm x 515 mm. Management noted  most
of the capacity in T aiwan is allocated to AMD, which it will start from the  game-console

products and then target to enter server CPU. For the Broadcom business, PTI has
previously announced a JV with Broadcom in Singapore, where it will invest US$400mn
into the JV for 30% stake, after receiving regulatory approvals in Q426. The JV will focus
on Broadcom's next generation networking products and will help it to enter into optical
communication field.  It has not disclosed the total investment amount and planned
capacity for the Singapore JV fab, but we believe the planned capacity  could be 2-3x

versus its scale in T aiwan. The JV is expected to start mass production from 2028 and PTI
will be in charged of the fab operations. Nevertheless, PTI also acknowledges that
TSMC's CoPoS will not only lead to potential competition, but could  also have some
impact on the equipment supply schedule.
Memory-backed price hike should continue
PTI's August sales of NT$8,558mn was up 3.5% MoM and 24.5% YoY. QTD sales is
tracking to 69%/68% of UBSe/Street estimates of +5%/+6% QoQ. Management noted

its  back-end loading is already at 90% and it has been selective on customer programs
for better ASP products. Management also expects back-end ASP should continue to
increase, driven primarily by higher labor costs and rising component prices. At the same
time, the pressure to lower its ASPs and  share the profit from yield improvement has
eased. Relative to peers, PTI noted it has been less aggressive in implementing price
increases, but has increased the frequency of price negotiation with its customer. As a

result, its gross margin for  memory packaging and test should continue to trend higher
in the coming quarters.
Capex will remain elevated in 2026-27F
Management reiterate its 2026F capex guidance of NT$50bn and believes its capex in
2027F will remain elevated. It aims to use its internal cash flow and bank loans to
support the capex, and remains committed to keep its  dividend payout at ~60%. Full
year  revenue for 2026F is likely to surpass its prior peak in 2022 (i.e. NT$83.9bn),

although it believes  market expectation of NT$100bn in 2026E could be too stretched.
Valuation: Retain NEUTRAL with PT of NT$260
We retain NEUTRAL on PTI with PT of NT$260, based on 17x 2027 PE. We will continue
to monitor its progress on FOPLP/CPO and price improvement sustainability.

Company Description
Powertech is a semiconductor back-end supplier with 75-80% of its sales from DRAM and
NAND packaging and testing and has been growing its logic back-end business.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Powertech: We value Powertech using PE methodology.
Upside/downside risks: 1) A stronger/weaker pricing environment; 2) slower-than expected

tech end demand and memory market recovery; and 3) faster/slower ramp of FOPLP and CPO
businesses.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

## Document 087: 260920_ms_CCL

Source PDF: all report pdf/260920_ms_CCL.pdf
Report source: line報告備份

### Page 1

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
CCL –  Doosan announces
capacity expansion in Korea and
China
What’s new? According to Business Korea, Doosan (000150.KS, not covered)
announced a KRW970B CCL capacity expansion on Sep 17:
• KRW420B in Korea to add optical module CCL lines, and
• KRW550B in China to build a new plant for AI accelerator and network
switch CCL.
Spending is phased through 2028, with the new lines coming online sequentially

from 2H28.
Our thoughts:  Our checks suggest this investment could add ~2,000K sheets per
month of CCL capacity at Doosan, equivalent to ~2% of global CCL capacity in 2028,
on our estimates. We view the investment as further evidence of robust demand for
high-end CCL and do not believe it undermines our thesis of continued high-end CCL
scarcity (see our CCL initiation report). As such, we see a positive read-across for
EMC, TUC, and Shengyi. We remain OW on EMC and TUC, while maintaining our EW

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
September 20, 2026 07:52 PM GMT

Valuation Methodology and Risks
                        Elite Material Co. (2383.TW)                    
Base case, derived from a residual income (RI) model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity 
of 9.3% (beta of 1.0, equity premium of 8.7% and risk-free rate of 1%), medium-term growth 
rate of 12%, and terminal growth rate of 3%.
                        Risks to Upside                    
n Stronger AI demand
n Share gains
n Faster-than-expected material upgrades
n Slower-than-expected CPO adoption
                        Risks to Downside                    
n Weaker AI demand
n Share loss

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 089: 260921_daiwa_winway

Source PDF: all report pdf/260921_daiwa_winway.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-21
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide an update on Winway’s business outlook.

What’s the impact: Marked revenue upside, but a more conservative 
gross margin outlook. We raise our 3Q26 revenue forecast by 9.3% to 
TWD5.0bn (+177.8% YoY) to reflect the stronger-than-expected execution 
of capacity expansion at Renwu Phase 1. Management guided for flat QoQ 
revenue in 4Q26, due to: 1) seasonality; 2) existing capacity being nearly 
fully utilised, with additional capacity scheduled to come online in 1H27; 
and 3) potential revenue recognition delays into 1H27 to accommodate 
customers’ schedules. On profitability, we lower our 3Q26E gross margin 
by 1.8pp, as we expect higher revenue contribution from probe cards and 
incremental depreciation from the new plant to dilute margins. Overall, for 
2026E and 2027E, we raise our revenue by 3.2% and 0.9%, while lowering 
our gross margin by 1.2pp and 1.7pp, respectively.

Probe card share concerns appear overdone. In response to concerns 
over probe card order losses, we believe the expanding TAM will attract 
competitors; the key differentiator is whether suppliers can scale capacity 
to meet customers’ demands. Winway does not expect its probe card share 
at its largest customer to decline and remains confident in maintaining its 
market-leading position. Its Micro-Electro-Mechanical Systems (MEMS) 
probe card supply agreement with Technoprobe (TPRO IM, Not Rated) 
remains in place, which should support its technology competitiveness.

Socket capacity expansion remains on track. Winway doubled its socket 
capacity YoY in 2026 and remains on track to reach monthly pogo-pin 
capacity of 14m units by 1H27, per management. We estimate monthly 
capacity to grow further to 16-18m units by end-2027. Management’s long-
term target is to raise its pogo-pin self-sufficiency ratio to 50-60%, which 
should support gross margin expansion through greater vertical integration.

What we recommend: We slightly raise revenue top-line but lower our 
2026-28E EPS by 1-5% to factor in conservative gross margin 
assumptions. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call, with 12M TP of TWD10,000, 
based on a PER of 50x, applied to our 2027E forward EPS. Our 2026-28E 
EPS CAGR of 100% makes our target PER undemanding, given that the 
resulting PEG ratio is 0.5x. Key downside risks: intensifying competition.

How we differ: Our revised 2026-27E EPS are now roughly in line with 
consensus, reflecting a more prudent gross margin. While market noise 
persists, we see no structural threat to Winway's leading position in sockets 
and probe cards.

21 September 2026

Capacity ramp drives revenue upside

➢ Revenue upside with more prudent gross margin assumptions 
➢ Probe card position intact; socket expansion on track 
➢ Reaffirming Buy (1) rating with an unchanged 12M TP of TWD10,000

Company profile 
Founded in Kaohsiung in 2001, WinWay has evolved from an optoelectronic test-fixture supplier into a 
leading logic test-interface provider. Its portfolio includes test sockets, MEMS probe cards and contact 
elements. Growth is driven by increasing AI/HPC testing complexity, GPU and ASIC design wins, 
expanding SLT adoption and next-generation HyperSocket solutions.

### Page 2

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
2
Strategy and positioning: Management launched PhotonLink as an architecture-
agnostic integrated optics platform covering CPO, NPO, and chip-to-chip optical
connections. It spans the full optical chain: Generating the light, shaping and
guiding it, and detecting it at the far end. Management discussed that its
customers asked it to supply complete assemblies rather than individual
components as stitching together lasers, fiber and passive optics is difficult. It

argued that no other company holds the full portfolio in-house and that Coherent
can manufacture at datacenter scale. Management expects a mix of adoption
paths, with some customers going straight to CPO and others starting with NPO
and moving to CPO later.
Figure 1. COHR's Portfolio of Optical AI Networking Products
Source: COHR at ECOC
Customers and content: In a full-solution example (combining the ELS, the
assembly to the switch or XPU, and the assembly out to the faceplate),

management said Coherent content could reach up to $15k of content per
switch/processor chip needing 100T of I/O capacity at 200G optical lane. Coherent
counts more than 10 customer engagements each in CPO and NPO and more than
5 in chip-to-chip, with activity going up sharply over the past six months. For CPO,
management said it has an anchor customer with an LTA secured, which it
identified as NVDA. It also has an anchor customer and LTA for NPO, which it did

not name. Separately, management cited two LTAs for ultra-high-power CW lasers
with leading hyperscale or AI datacenter customers, also unnamed.

### Page 3

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
3
Figure 2. PhontonLink Platform
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026
Timing: CPO for scale-out, tied to the NVIDIA agreement, begins ramping in the
December quarter (calendar Q4 2026). CPO for scale-up follows in the second half
of calendar 2027. NPO ramps in the second half of next calendar year, mainly for
scale-up. Chip-to-chip is further out, around 2029-2030.
Light generation: The ultra-high-power CW laser sits in an external laser source

that holds up to eight lasers, each feeding roughly four links. It starts ramping this
quarter, with significant volume next year, on the 6-inch InP platform in Sherman,
Texas. For NPO, a high-power buried-heterostructure CW laser is already shipping
in millions of units per month for pluggables. VCSEL arrays come in a conventional
version for ribbon fiber and a high-density 2D version that can be flip-chipped
directly to drivers and TIAs with backside lenses. Photodetectors run to 200G

today, with 400G in development.
Figure 3. UHP CW Laser Leader
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026

### Page 4

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
4
Fab footprint: Sherman runs 6-inch InP and GaAs, Sweden runs 6-inch InP . Zurich
runs 6-inch GaAs and is adding 6-inch InP for high-power lasers, while Fremont
runs a 3-inch line. GaAs capacity has grown fourfold since the 6-inch move about
six years ago, with capacity for over one billion VCSEL arrays per year. InP capacity
doubled last year, a quarter ahead of schedule, and management plans to double it

again or more.
Figure 4. COHR Capacity Expansion
Source: COHR at ECOC 2026
Shape, guide, and receive: Coherent grows garnet in the United States and says it
supplies a significant fraction of the industry's garnet and isolators, with
customers signing LTAs for capacity. The ELS is built on the transceiver line and is
sampling now. The rest of the portfolio covers specialty fiber (polarization-
maintaining fiber production-ready, mode-matched fiber introduced at ECOC,

multi-core fiber), micro-lens arrays, V-grooves, fiber array units, a detachable
connector, circulators, WDM multiplexers, and fiber shuffles.
Silicon photonics and engines: Management discussed Coherent having an in-
house silicon photonics design team, uses foundries for wafers, and does assembly
and test in-house. It ships 200G-per-lane silicon photonics in transceivers today
and showed a 400G link at OFC with a post-deadline paper. It has a 6.4T silicon

photonics NPO module (32 lanes of 200G) and a VCSEL-based NPO with 2D
arrays, which management says achieves about 1.2 pJ/bit including the laser.
Architecture trade-off: Management said NPO is easier to deploy and service but
costs power and signal integrity versus CPO, and that a common NPO socket can
take silicon photonics or VCSELs. It positioned Coherent as an honest advisor
because it can compare all the options. Chip-to-chip is a growing topic, with WDM

and circulators important there.

Key takeaways from Citi discussion with management:
What is Coherent's competitive advantage in 3.2T, and will it participate?
Management said it has already demonstrated the building blocks for the 3.2T
generation, including 400G-per-lane EMLs and 400G silicon photonics, and
expects to participate just as it enables 1.6T today. Management showed these

demonstrations as early as two years ago.
What is the gross margin path, and what offsets mix pressure? Management
mentioned that the 42%+ target was deliberately open-ended and that it expects
to exceed it. It cautioned against expecting a step change over the next two to
three quarters. Fast transceiver growth is a mix headwind, offset by overhead
absorption, yield gains and factory consolidation. Management expects the 800G-
to-1.6T transition to be margin-accretive, with the cutover forecast around 2028.

### Page 6

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
6
OCS, shipping for two to three quarters, is the first of the new platforms to
generate revenue. OCS, CPO and multi-rail will contribute more to margins as mix
builds.
What are the challenges and economics of the 6-inch InP transition?
Management said it announced 6-inch capability in March 2024 after years of
work with substrate vendors that began before the AI boom. Output is expected to
be roughly 50/50 between 3-inch and 6-inch by the end of the quarter, with all

incremental capacity on 6-inch, reaching about 75% by the end of 2027. A 6-inch
die costs about half as much per yielded die, and each line delivers about four
times the capacity. Yields are better on modern tooling. Management cited a three-
to-four-year lead over competitors and secured substrate supply from five
suppliers across three continents. It also credited its 6-inch GaAs VCSEL line to a
large consumer-electronics customer that demanded the larger wafer size.

Will Coherent keep external InP suppliers? COHR only sources EMLs externally
and that it will keep some external supply for continuity on a critical component.
How does Coherent differ from Chinese laser suppliers? Management argued
that module assembly is easier to replicate but high-speed lasers are not. It said
there are no high-speed EMLs or broadband tunable lasers from China, and that
Chinese CW lasers top out around 70-100 mW. It sees only one InP laser

competitor worth naming and expects new Chinese module entrants to take share
from existing Chinese suppliers rather than from Coherent.
Does NPO vs CPO matter for content, and can low-power lasers serve NPO?
Management said it does not matter much, because the components and dollar
content are similar. Designs are custom and take 18 to 24 months to reach volume,
and management reiterated scale-up production revenue in the second half of next
year. Management rejected the view that NPO can use low-power lasers, arguing

that coupling several weak lasers loses light and defeats the power-efficiency
purpose.
What is multi-rail, and how is it progressing? Multi-rail addresses scale-across
links between data centers. Management said it delivers four times the capacity in
the same footprint at roughly 30% lower power per lane, enabled by a multi-chip
uncooled pump that it says has no comparable competitor. Small shipments are
underway and production shipments begin in the first half of next calendar year. It

sells at the component, module, line-card and system level, including white-label
to OEMs.

### Page 7

Coherent Corp (COHR.K)
21 September 2026 Citi Research
7
Coherent Corp
Company description
COHR is a vertically integrated manufacturing company that develops
engineered materials and opto-electronic components for industrial
materials processing, communications, aerospace and defense, consumer
electronics, semiconductor capital equipment, life sciences and automotive
end markets. COHR produces a wide variety of application-specific photonic
and electronic materials and components, and deploys them in various forms,

including integration with advanced software. COHR's customer base
includes original equipment manufacturers, laser end users, system
integrators of high-power lasers, manufacturers of equipment and devices for
industrial, optical communications, consumer electronics, security and
monitoring applications, U.S. government prime contractors, and various U.S.
government agencies. COHR is a headquartered in Pennsylvania.
Investment strategy
We are Buy-rated on COHR. We believe that the stock's multiple will benefit

from the AI appeal while its fundamentals should expand on new
management's strong execution and focus on profitability.
Valuation
We value COHR at $420 or 30x forward P/E applied to FY2028 EPS of ~$16
discounted back. Our COHR valuation is nevertheless at a 10% discount to
the average forward P/E of AI optics peers to reflect a lower AI sales mix and
3-year EPS CAGR vs peers such as LITE.
Risks
Downside risks to the achievement of our target price include: 1) increased

competition on AI and SiC leads to limited market share growth and limited
revenue upside, 2) inability to achieve synergies and margin targets
established post acquisition of Finisar, and 3) prolonged economic recession
leads to slower 5G build-out and lower consumer demand driving revenue of
COHR down.
Upside risks to the achievement of our target price include: 1) gaining share in
AI and SiC market through new product offerings and first to market status

on larger wafer sizes, 2) quicker and stronger adoption of AI workloads,
electric and autonomous vehicles, and 3) quicker return of margins to pre-
Finisar acquisition levels.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do

## Document 096: 260922_ubs_GUC
